^SAMPLE = GSM5897980
!Sample_title = mESC_TRE_3FLAG_Obox4_IgG
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM5897980
!Sample_status = Public on Aug 29 2023
!Sample_submission_date = Feb 14 2022
!Sample_last_update_date = Aug 29 2023
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Obox4 inducible mESC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = background strain: 129/Ola
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: culture cell (ESC)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: N/A
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: doxycycline
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = genomic DNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover.  For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_description = CUT&RUN-enriched DNA
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: CTU&RUN
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.
!Sample_data_processing = Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.
!Sample_data_processing = For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: RNA-seq reads (.csv)
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: CUT&RUN peaks (.narrowPeak or .broadPeak)
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: CUT&RUN read coverage (.bigwig)

!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = genomic DNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover.  For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_description = CUT&RUN-enriched DNA
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: CTU&RUN
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.
!Sample_data_processing = Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.
!Sample_data_processing = For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: RNA-seq reads (.csv)
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: CUT&RUN peaks (.narrowPeak or .broadPeak)
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: CUT&RUN read coverage (.bigwig)

!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = genomic DNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover.  For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_description = CUT&RUN-enriched DNA
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: CTU&RUN
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.
!Sample_data_processing = Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.
!Sample_data_processing = For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = genomic DNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover.  For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_description = CUT&RUN-enriched DNA
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: CTU&RUN
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.
!Sample_data_processing = Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.
!Sample_data_processing = For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover.  For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_description = CUT&RUN-enriched DNA
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: CTU&RUN
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.
!Sample_data_processing = Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.
!Sample_data_processing = For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Obox4 KO mESC

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: culture cell (ESC)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: N/A
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: N/A
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover.  For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_description = CUT&RUN-enriched DNA
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: CTU&RUN
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.
!Sample_data_processing = Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.
!Sample_data_processing = For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: RNA-seq reads (.csv)
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: CUT&RUN peaks (.narrowPeak or .broadPeak)
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: CUT&RUN read coverage (.bigwig)

!Sample_supplementary_file_1 = ftp://ftp.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/samples/GSM5898nnn/GSM5898000/suppl/GSM5898000_mESC_Obox4_KO_PlaB_minus_H3K27Ac_rep3.narrowPeak.gz
!Sample_supplementary_file_2 = ftp://ftp.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/samples/GSM5898nnn/GSM5898000/suppl/GSM5898000_mESC_Obox4_KO_PlaB_minus_H3K27ac_rep3.bigwig
!Sample_series_id = GSE196671
!Sample_data_row_count = 0
^SAMPLE = GSM5898001
!Sample_title = mESC_Obox4_KO_PlaB_minus_H3K27me3_rep1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM5898001
!Sample_status = Public on Aug 29 2023
!Sample_submission_date = Feb 14 2022
!Sample_last_update_date = Aug 29 2023
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Obox4 KO mESC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = background strain: 129/Ola
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: culture cell (ESC)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: N/A
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: N/A
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).

For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_description = CUT&RUN-enriched DNA
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: CTU&RUN
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.
!Sample_data_processing = Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.
!Sample_data_processing = For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = EB3 mESC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = background strain: 129/Ola
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: culture cell (ESC)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: N/A
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: pladienolide B
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).

For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_description = CUT&RUN-enriched DNA
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: CTU&RUN
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.
!Sample_data_processing = Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.
!Sample_data_processing = For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: RNA-seq reads (.csv)
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: CUT&RUN peaks (.narrowPeak or .broadPeak)

!Sample_supplementary_file_2 = ftp://ftp.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/samples/GSM5898nnn/GSM5898008/suppl/GSM5898008_mESC_WT_PlaB_plus_H3K27ac_rep1.bigwig

^SAMPLE = GSM5898009
!Sample_title = mESC_WT_PlaB_plus_H3K27ac_rep2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM5898009
!Sample_status = Public on Aug 29 2023
!Sample_submission_date = Feb 14 2022
!Sample_last_update_date = Aug 29 2023
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = EB3 mESC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = background strain: 129/Ola
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: culture cell (ESC)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: N/A
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: pladienolide B
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = genomic DNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover.  For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.

The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_description = CUT&RUN-enriched DNA
!Sample_data_processing = Library strategy: CTU&RUN
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.
!Sample_data_processing = Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.
!Sample_data_processing = For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = EB3 mESC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = background strain: 129/Ola
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: culture cell (ESC)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: N/A
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: pladienolide B
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = genomic DNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover.  For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.

!Sample_data_processing = For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Obox4 KO mESC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = background strain: 129/Ola
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: culture cell (ESC)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: N/A
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: pladienolide B
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = genomic DNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover.  For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.

Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs.  For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.
!Sample_data_processing = For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.
!Sample_data_processing = For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Obox4 KO mESC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: culture cell (ESC)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: N/A
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: pladienolide B
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = genomic DNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover.  For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.  For CUT&RUN,  enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.

For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.  For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.  For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_source_name_ch1 = culture cell
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: culture cell
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: ESC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: TRE-3xFLAG-Obox4
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: N/A
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = background strain: 129/Ola
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution. For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover. For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For CUT&RUN, enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.  Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.  For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. !Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution. For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover. For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For CUT&RUN, enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.  Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.  For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs. For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.  For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.  For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_title = RNAseq_ESC_Obox4_OE_48hr_rep1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM6670791
!Sample_status = Public on Aug 29 2023
!Sample_submission_date = Oct 21 2022
!Sample_last_update_date = Aug 29 2023
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = culture cell
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: culture cell
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: ESC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: TRE-3xFLAG-Obox4
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: doxycycline
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = background strain: 129/Ola
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).

After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution. For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover. For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For CUT&RUN, enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.  Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.  For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs. For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.  For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.  For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_library_strategy = RNA-Seq
!Sample_relation = BioSample: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/biosample/SAMN31408512
!Sample_relation = SRA: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/sra?term=SRX17992755
!Sample_supplementary_file_1 = NONE
!Sample_series_id = GSE196671
!Sample_data_row_count = 0
^SAMPLE = GSM6670800
!Sample_title = RNAseq_2C_rescue_KD_rep1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM6670800
!Sample_status = Public on Aug 29 2023
!Sample_submission_date = Oct 21 2022
!Sample_last_update_date = Aug 29 2023
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = emrbyo
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: emrbyo
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: 2C embryo
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: WT
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: Dux/Obox4 double KD rescued by Obox4 mRNA
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = background strain: B6D2F1
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution. For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover. For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For CUT&RUN, enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.  Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.  For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs. For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.  For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.  For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.  Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.  For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs. For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.  For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.  For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.

!Sample_title = RNAseq_morula_double_KD_rep2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM6670805
!Sample_status = Public on Aug 29 2023
!Sample_submission_date = Oct 21 2022
!Sample_last_update_date = Aug 29 2023
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = emrbyo
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: emrbyo
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: morula
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: WT
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: Dux/Obox4 double KD
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = background strain: B6D2F1
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = 8-weeks-old BDF1 female mice were injected with 150µL CARD HyperOva (Kyudo Co., Ltd.). After 48 hours, the female mice were injected with 5IU hCG (ASKA Animal Health Co., Ltd.) and mated with BDF1 male mice. After 22 hours, PN3 zygotes were collected. Cumulus cells were removed by briefly culture the zygotes in KSOM medium (Merck, MR-101-D) supplemented with 0.3µg/µL hyaluronidase (Merck, H4272). Embryos were cultured in KSOM medium drops covered with liquid paraffin (Nacalai Tesque, 26137-85) under 5% CO2 at 37°C. After 4 hours, PBS containing either 40µM scramble ASO, 20µM anti-Dux ASO, 20µM anti-Obox4 ASO, 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture, or 40µM equal molar anti-Dux/anti-Obox4 ASO mixture with 100ng/μL codon-optimized Obox4 mRNA were microinjected into male pronuclei of zygotes using FemtoJet 4i microinjector (Eppendorf, 5252000013).
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Eight-weeks old female and male B6D2F1 (BDF1) mice were purchased from the Japan SLC, Inc. All animal experiments were reviewed and approved by the Institutional Animal Care and Use Committee (protocol no. 09105-(10) and 11045-(6)) at Keio University, where mice were maintained and fed ad libitum with standard diet and water in a temperature- and light-controlled room (23℃±3℃, 14/10 hour light/dark cycle).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution. For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover. For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For CUT&RUN, enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, 30 middle-2C embryos were collected 20 hours after microinjection in each independent biological replicates. Zona pellucida was removed by treating the embryos with Acidic Tyrode’s solution. For RNA-seq of culture cells, total RNA was prepared using RNeasy Kit according to the manufacturer’s instruction. The RNA solution was subjected to TURBO DNase treatment according to the manufacturer’s instructions to remove genomic DNA carryover. For CUT&RUN, 1×105 culture cells freshly prepared by trypsin digestion were used to perform CUT&RUN assay with CUT&RUN Assay Kit (Cell Signaling Technology, 86652) according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For RNA-seq of embryos, libraries were constructed with SMART-Seq Stranded Kit (TaKaRa, 634442) and indexed with SMARTer RNA Unique Dual Index Kit (TaKaRa, 634451) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For RNA-seq of culture cells, libraries were constructed using 1µg total RNA with NEBNext Ultra II Directional RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7760L) and indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, E6440S) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. For CUT&RUN, enriched DNAs were subjected to library construction using NEBNext Ultra II DNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, E7645L) with the adapter ligation step performed at 50°C instead of 65°C to prevent denature of small DNA inserts. The libraries were indexed with NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina according to the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_data_processing = Base calling was performed using bcl2fastq v2.20 to generate raw reads.  Quality control and adapter trimming was done using fastp (Chen et al., 2018) v0.23.2.  For RNA-seq, quality controlled reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using STAR (Dobin et al., 2013) v2.7.9a with default arguments. Reads were counted against GRCm38.p6 comprehensive gene annotation (Frankish et al., 2020) and mm10 repeats from UCSC RepeatMasker using FeatureCounts (Liao et al., 2014) v2.0.1 with multi mapping reads discard for non-TE features and count fractionally for TEs. For CUT&RUN-seq, quality controller reads were aligned to GRCm38.p6 reference genome using Bowtie2 (Langmead and Salzberg, 2012) v2.4.1 with default arguments.  For CUT&RUN, Peaks were called in each biological replicated using MACS3 (Zhang et al., 2008) v3.0.0a6 callpeak function (-f BAMPE). Quantitative histone modification analysis with replicated samples was performed using MAnorm2 (Tu et al., 2021) v1.2.0.  For visualization of CUT&RUN results, alignment tracks were generated using deepTools (Ramírez et al., 2016) v3.5.1 bamCoverage function (--binSize 10 --normalizeusing CPM --smoothLength 30). Visualization of genomic tracks was performed using trackplot (Pohl and Beato, 2014) v1.3.10.
!Sample_platform_id = GPL24247
